| 21100 | Basic salary deduction | Basic salary deduction | Basic salary deduction. Flag class 1 (reversal deduction): Yes means the signed amount reduces that base. Deduction that reverses part of earning 62100 (Basic salary): it reduces the same bases as the earning, so the flags are those of the earning family; the parameters sit on anchor 62100. A recovery of an advance already paid is not a reduction of wages. | 21610 | Health insurance & Life insurance contributions | Health insurance & Life insurance contributions - type 1 | Health insurance & Life insurance contributions - type 1. Employee contribution to health or life insurance (flag class 2, salary reduction through a section 125 plan: No means removed from the base before the tax). Through a section 125 plan: FIT, FICA and FUTA excluded [US-FEDS5-008]; Benefit chosen under a section 125 cafeteria plan: if the employee chooses cash or another taxable benefit, it is subject to all employment taxes; if the employee chooses a nontaxable (qualified) benefit, the treatment is the same as if the benefit were provided outside the plan. [US-FEDS5-029]. Outside a section 125 plan the contribution is paid from taxed wages and does not reduce any base. HSA through a section 125 plan: excluded for a qualified individual up to the HSA contribution limit (employer or section 125 salary-reduction contributions) [US-FEDS5-008]; limits 4,400 USD and 8,750 USD [US-FED-024]. The monthly employee share of the lowest-cost self-only coverage feeds US-032. | 21640 | Pensions & Pension Contributions (pre-tax deduction) | Pre-tax 401(k) elective deferral - employee deduction | Pre-tax 401(k) elective deferral - employee deduction. Employee pre-tax 401(k) elective deferral (flag class 2, salary reduction: No means removed from the base before the tax): excluded (up to the section 402(g) limit) for federal income tax [US-FEDS5-029]; for social security and Medicare included [US-FEDS5-029]; for FUTA included [US-FEDS5-029]. The deferral reduces the box 1 wages only. Reported in the annual wage statement box 12. Limits (2026): elective deferral 24,500 USD, catch-up from age 50 8,000 USD, catch-up at ages 60 to 63 11,250 USD [US-FED-023]. Per-State departures: Pennsylvania taxable when made (employer contributions not) [US-PAS5-011]; New Jersey 401(k) excluded up to federal limit [US-NJS5-006]; unemployment insurance, Alaska excluded (departs from FUTA) [US-AKS5-005] and Rhode Island Employee contributions to retirement plans such as 401(k), medical or disability insurance plans and Section 125 cafeteria plans are excluded from wages under the RI Employment Security and Temporary Disability Insurance Acts [US-RIS5-017]; the other States are on their rows. | 21900 | Custom Social Security (employee) | Social security tax (OASDI) - employee | Social security tax (OASDI) - employee. Social security tax (OASDI) withheld from the employee: 6.2% of social security wages up to the annual wage base 184,500 USD (effective 2026-01-01 to 2026-12-31); 192,300 USD (effective 2027-01-01 to 2027-12-31) per employee per employer [US-FEDS5-005]. Computation: the lesser of (remaining annual maximum) and (rate x period social security wages), never annualised and divided. The employer share is US-005. Accumulator: year-to-date social security wages (Subject to Social Insurance Q1 plus the year-to-date mechanism). Exempt: Exempt: pay of a nonresident alien in F-1, J-1, M-1 or Q status for services that carry out the purpose of admission, on or off campus, including authorized Curricular and Optional Practical Training. Not exempt: other employment of the same person (e.g. an unrelated part-time job), and any pay once the person is a resident alien. [US-FEDS5-016]. Declared: W-2 box 4; Form 941 line 5a. | 62100 | Basic salary | Basic salary | Basic salary. Salary or hourly wages for the period: cash remuneration for services, wages for every federal tax. Federal income tax (FIT) is withheld by IRS Pub. 15-T (2026) Worksheet 1A with the Annual Percentage Method tables (STANDARD schedule; Step-2-checked schedule when W-4 Step 2 box checked) [US-FEDS5-001]. Rounding: Optional: wages may be rounded to the nearest dollar and the period tax rounded to the nearest dollar (drop under 50 cents, 50-99 cents up) [US-FED-001]. Social security (OASDI): employee 6.2% and employer 6.2% of wages up to the annual wage base 184,500 USD (effective 2026-01-01 to 2026-12-31); 192,300 USD (effective 2027-01-01 to 2027-12-31) per employee per employer [US-FEDS5-005]. Medicare: employee 1.45% and employer 1.45% of all wages [US-FEDS5-005]. Additional Medicare Tax: 0.9% withheld from the employee on wages above 200,000 USD in the calendar year [US-FEDS5-005]. FUTA (employer only): net rate 0.6% on the first 7,000 USD of wages per employee per year [US-FED-007]. State income tax, State unemployment insurance, State programme contributions and local taxes follow the jurisdiction rows of this catalogue (see the State layer index on the Global WTC Coverage sheet); a State departure from the federal base is stated on the row that carries it. Inputs: Rate-of-pay basis, Pay frequency, Full-time or part-time status. The premium portion of qualified overtime is reported in W-2 box 12 code Total amount of qualified overtime compensation [US-FEDS5-007]; deduction cap for qualified overtime 12,500 USD (25,000 USD married filing jointly) [US-FEDS5-030]. State treatment: no State reduces withholding for it; the State rows list the assessment-level subtractions. Federal income tax (FIT) is withheld by IRS Pub. 15-T (2026) Worksheet 1A with the Annual Percentage Method tables (STANDARD schedule; Step-2-checked schedule when W-4 Step 2 box checked) [US-FEDS5-001]. Rounding: Optional: wages may be rounded to the nearest dollar and the period tax rounded to the nearest dollar (drop under 50 cents, 50-99 cents up) [US-FED-001]. Social security (OASDI): employee 6.2% and employer 6.2% of wages up to the annual wage base 184,500 USD (effective 2026-01-01 to 2026-12-31); 192,300 USD (effective 2027-01-01 to 2027-12-31) per employee per employer [US-FEDS5-005]. Medicare: employee 1.45% and employer 1.45% of all wages [US-FEDS5-005]. Additional Medicare Tax: 0.9% withheld from the employee on wages above 200,000 USD in the calendar year [US-FEDS5-005]. FUTA (employer only): net rate 0.6% on the first 7,000 USD of wages per employee per year [US-FED-007]. | 62140 | Salary addition | Salary addition - type 1 | Salary addition - type 1. Pay added to the base salary of the period (allowance paid with salary, differential, supplementary pay): cash remuneration for services, wages for every federal tax. Federal income tax (FIT) is withheld by IRS Pub. 15-T (2026) Worksheet 1A with the Annual Percentage Method tables (STANDARD schedule; Step-2-checked schedule when W-4 Step 2 box checked) [US-FEDS5-001]. Rounding: Optional: wages may be rounded to the nearest dollar and the period tax rounded to the nearest dollar (drop under 50 cents, 50-99 cents up) [US-FED-001]. Social security (OASDI): employee 6.2% and employer 6.2% of wages up to the annual wage base 184,500 USD (effective 2026-01-01 to 2026-12-31); 192,300 USD (effective 2027-01-01 to 2027-12-31) per employee per employer [US-FEDS5-005]. Medicare: employee 1.45% and employer 1.45% of all wages [US-FEDS5-005]. Additional Medicare Tax: 0.9% withheld from the employee on wages above 200,000 USD in the calendar year [US-FEDS5-005]. | 62150 | Premium pay (i.e. Shift premium) | Premium pay (i.e. Shift premium) - type 1 | Premium pay (i.e. Shift premium) - type 1. Premium pay (shift, night, weekend or hazard premium): cash remuneration for services, wages for every federal tax. The premium of qualified overtime is reported through the overtime rows. Federal income tax (FIT) is withheld by IRS Pub. 15-T (2026) Worksheet 1A with the Annual Percentage Method tables (STANDARD schedule; Step-2-checked schedule when W-4 Step 2 box checked) [US-FEDS5-001]. Rounding: Optional: wages may be rounded to the nearest dollar and the period tax rounded to the nearest dollar (drop under 50 cents, 50-99 cents up) [US-FED-001]. Social security (OASDI): employee 6.2% and employer 6.2% of wages up to the annual wage base 184,500 USD (effective 2026-01-01 to 2026-12-31); 192,300 USD (effective 2027-01-01 to 2027-12-31) per employee per employer [US-FEDS5-005]. Medicare: employee 1.45% and employer 1.45% of all wages [US-FEDS5-005]. Additional Medicare Tax: 0.9% withheld from the employee on wages above 200,000 USD in the calendar year [US-FEDS5-005]. | 62200 | Bonus (annual, referral, etc) | Bonus (annual, referral, etc) - type 1 | Bonus (annual, referral, etc) - type 1. Bonus-type supplemental wages (annual, referral, profit sharing, discretionary or one-time): wages for every federal tax. Federal income tax withholding: optional flat rate 22% [US-FEDS5-003] (US-002) or the aggregate method (US-001); mandatory rate 37% above 1,000,000 USD of supplemental wages in the year [US-FEDS5-003] (US-003). Social security (OASDI): employee 6.2% and employer 6.2% of wages up to the annual wage base 184,500 USD (effective 2026-01-01 to 2026-12-31); 192,300 USD (effective 2027-01-01 to 2027-12-31) per employee per employer [US-FEDS5-005]. Medicare: employee 1.45% and employer 1.45% of all wages [US-FEDS5-005]. Additional Medicare Tax: 0.9% withheld from the employee on wages above 200,000 USD in the calendar year [US-FEDS5-005]. FUTA (employer only): net rate 0.6% on the first 7,000 USD of wages per employee per year [US-FED-007]. State income tax, State unemployment insurance, State programme contributions and local taxes follow the jurisdiction rows of this catalogue (see the State layer index on the Global WTC Coverage sheet); a State departure from the federal base is stated on the row that carries it. The State flat rates and aggregate-only States for supplemental wages are on the State income tax rows. Each code of the family is one type of the same payment. | Earning |  | US-TX-02 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes-No | Yes-No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | NA |  |  | Yes | Yes |  | US-FED-007, US-FEDP52-005, US-FEDS5-001, US-FEDS5-003, US-FEDS5-005, US-MULTIS5-017 | IRS Publication 15 (2026) Employer's Tax Guide - What's New: supplemental wage rates, backup withholding, information-return threshold, household/election worker thresholds (IRS) [US-FEDS5-003] |

| 62300 | Annual leave pay | Annual leave pay | Annual leave pay. Annual leave (vacation) pay and the payout of accrued leave: wages for every federal tax when paid. Federal income tax (FIT) is withheld by IRS Pub. 15-T (2026) Worksheet 1A with the Annual Percentage Method tables (STANDARD schedule; Step-2-checked schedule when W-4 Step 2 box checked) [US-FEDS5-001]. Rounding: Optional: wages may be rounded to the nearest dollar and the period tax rounded to the nearest dollar (drop under 50 cents, 50-99 cents up) [US-FED-001]. Social security (OASDI): employee 6.2% and employer 6.2% of wages up to the annual wage base 184,500 USD (effective 2026-01-01 to 2026-12-31); 192,300 USD (effective 2027-01-01 to 2027-12-31) per employee per employer [US-FEDS5-005]. Medicare: employee 1.45% and employer 1.45% of all wages [US-FEDS5-005]. Additional Medicare Tax: 0.9% withheld from the employee on wages above 200,000 USD in the calendar year [US-FEDS5-005]. FUTA (employer only): net rate 0.6% on the first 7,000 USD of wages per employee per year [US-FED-007]. Payout at separation depends on the written policy and State law: inputs Vacation forfeiture policy acknowledged in writing, Final wage payment date, Date of separation (termination date). Declared as wages in the pay period of payment. | Earning |  | US-TX-01 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | NA |  |  | Yes | Yes |  | US-FED-007, US-FEDP52-005, US-FEDS5-001, US-FEDS5-003, US-FEDS5-005, US-MULTIS5-017 | IRS Publication 15-T (2026) Federal Income Tax Withholding Methods - Worksheet 1A and Annual Percentage Method tables (Automated Payroll) (IRS) [US-FEDS5-001]; IRS Instructions for Form 941 (Rev. March 2026) - FICA rates and wage base, Additional Medicare Tax (IRS) [US-FEDS5-005]; SSA Contribution and Benefit Base page (SSA) [US-FEDB5-401]; Instructions for Form 940 (2025) (IRS) [US-FED-007] |

| 62310 | Expat allowance | Expat allowance - type 1 | Expat allowance - type 1. Expatriate or assignment allowance paid in cash: configured as cash remuneration, with the wage treatment of the regular wage rows. Not configured in this release (open gap WTC-RQ-01): allowance-specific exclusions are not configured. Nonresident alien employees: fringe and allowance sourcing rules are on US-033. Not configured in this release (open gap WTC-RQ-07): State unemployment insurance and programme definition. Federal income tax (FIT) is withheld by IRS Pub. 15-T (2026) Worksheet 1A with the Annual Percentage Method tables (STANDARD schedule; Step-2-checked schedule when W-4 Step 2 box checked) [US-FEDS5-001]. Rounding: Optional: wages may be rounded to the nearest dollar and the period tax rounded to the nearest dollar (drop under 50 cents, 50-99 cents up) [US-FED-001]. Social security (OASDI): employee 6.2% and employer 6.2% of wages up to the annual wage base 184,500 USD (effective 2026-01-01 to 2026-12-31); 192,300 USD (effective 2027-01-01 to 2027-12-31) per employee per employer [US-FEDS5-005]. Medicare: employee 1.45% and employer 1.45% of all wages [US-FEDS5-005]. Additional Medicare Tax: 0.9% withheld from the employee on wages above 200,000 USD in the calendar year [US-FEDS5-005]. | 62400 | Housing allowance | Housing allowance | Housing allowance. Housing, rent or lodging allowance paid in cash: configured as cash remuneration. Not configured in this release (open gap WTC-RQ-01): exclusion of lodging furnished for the convenience of the employer is not configured. Not configured in this release (open gap WTC-RQ-07): State unemployment insurance and programme definition. Federal income tax (FIT) is withheld by IRS Pub. 15-T (2026) Worksheet 1A with the Annual Percentage Method tables (STANDARD schedule; Step-2-checked schedule when W-4 Step 2 box checked) [US-FEDS5-001]. Rounding: Optional: wages may be rounded to the nearest dollar and the period tax rounded to the nearest dollar (drop under 50 cents, 50-99 cents up) [US-FED-001]. Social security (OASDI): employee 6.2% and employer 6.2% of wages up to the annual wage base 184,500 USD (effective 2026-01-01 to 2026-12-31); 192,300 USD (effective 2027-01-01 to 2027-12-31) per employee per employer [US-FEDS5-005]. Medicare: employee 1.45% and employer 1.45% of all wages [US-FEDS5-005]. Additional Medicare Tax: 0.9% withheld from the employee on wages above 200,000 USD in the calendar year [US-FEDS5-005]. FUTA (employer only): net rate 0.6% on the first 7,000 USD of wages per employee per year [US-FED-007]. | Earning |  | US-TX-01 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes-No | Yes-No | NA |  |  | Yes | Yes |  | US-FED-007, US-FEDP52-005, US-FEDS5-001, US-FEDS5-003, US-FEDS5-005, US-MULTIS5-017 | IRS Publication 15-T (2026) Federal Income Tax Withholding Methods - Worksheet 1A and Annual Percentage Method tables (Automated Payroll) (IRS) [US-FEDS5-001]; IRS Instructions for Form 941 (Rev. | 62500 | Car allowance | Car allowance | Car allowance. Car, car maintenance, fuel or car and transportation allowance paid in cash: configured as cash remuneration. Business mileage reimbursement is not carried: Not configured in this release (open gap WTC-RQ-89): the 2026 business standard mileage rate is not carried. Not configured in this release (open gap WTC-RQ-01): accountable plan treatment of vehicle reimbursement. Federal income tax (FIT) is withheld by IRS Pub. 15-T (2026) Worksheet 1A with the Annual Percentage Method tables (STANDARD schedule; Step-2-checked schedule when W-4 Step 2 box checked) [US-FEDS5-001]. Rounding: Optional: wages may be rounded to the nearest dollar and the period tax rounded to the nearest dollar (drop under 50 cents, 50-99 cents up) [US-FED-001]. Social security (OASDI): employee 6.2% and employer 6.2% of wages up to the annual wage base 184,500 USD (effective 2026-01-01 to 2026-12-31); 192,300 USD (effective 2027-01-01 to 2027-12-31) per employee per employer [US-FEDS5-005]. Medicare: employee 1.45% and employer 1.45% of all wages [US-FEDS5-005]. Additional Medicare Tax: 0.9% withheld from the employee on wages above 200,000 USD in the calendar year [US-FEDS5-005]. FUTA (employer only): net rate 0.6% on the first 7,000 USD of wages per employee per year [US-FED-007]. | Earning |  | US-TX-01 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes-No | Yes-No | NA |  |  | Yes | Yes |  | US-FED-007, US-FEDP52-005, US-FEDS5-001, US-FEDS5-003, US-FEDS5-005, US-MULTIS5-017 | IRS Publication 15-T (2026) Federal Income Tax Withholding Methods - Worksheet 1A and Annual Percentage Method tables (Automated Payroll) (IRS) [US-FEDS5-001]; IRS Instructions for Form 941 (Rev. | 62540 | Relocation allowance | Relocation allowance - type 1 | Relocation allowance - type 1. Relocation or moving allowance: moving expense exclusion applies: False [US-FEDS5-008], so the allowance is wages for every federal tax. Declared in W-2 box 1. Federal income tax (FIT) is withheld by IRS Pub. 15-T (2026) Worksheet 1A with the Annual Percentage Method tables (STANDARD schedule; Step-2-checked schedule when W-4 Step 2 box checked) [US-FEDS5-001]. Rounding: Optional: wages may be rounded to the nearest dollar and the period tax rounded to the nearest dollar (drop under 50 cents, 50-99 cents up) [US-FED-001]. Social security (OASDI): employee 6.2% and employer 6.2% of wages up to the annual wage base 184,500 USD (effective 2026-01-01 to 2026-12-31); 192,300 USD (effective 2027-01-01 to 2027-12-31) per employee per employer [US-FEDS5-005]. Medicare: employee 1.45% and employer 1.45% of all wages [US-FEDS5-005]. Additional Medicare Tax: 0.9% withheld from the employee on wages above 200,000 USD in the calendar year [US-FEDS5-005]. FUTA (employer only): net rate 0.6% on the first 7,000 USD of wages per employee per year [US-FED-007]. | 62610 | Medical insurance / Medical Care | Medical insurance / Medical Care - type 1 | Medical insurance / Medical Care - type 1. Medical insurance or medical care amount paid through the payroll. A benefit chosen under a section 125 plan: Benefit chosen under a section 125 cafeteria plan: if the employee chooses cash or another taxable benefit, it is subject to all employment taxes; if the employee chooses a nontaxable (qualified) benefit, the treatment is the same as if the benefit were provided outside the plan. [US-FEDS5-029]. Health coverage under a section 125 plan: FIT, FICA and FUTA excluded [US-FEDS5-008]. Cash paid in place of coverage is wages for every federal tax; the flags are conditional on whether the amount is a plan benefit or cash. Health salary reduction cap: 3,400 USD, carry-over 680 USD [US-FEDS5-010]. | 62650 | Social raise / Social allowance | Social raise / Social allowance | Social raise / Social allowance. Social allowance paid in cash: configured as cash remuneration, with the wage treatment of the regular wage rows. Federal income tax (FIT) is withheld by IRS Pub. 15-T (2026) Worksheet 1A with the Annual Percentage Method tables (STANDARD schedule; Step-2-checked schedule when W-4 Step 2 box checked) [US-FEDS5-001]. Rounding: Optional: wages may be rounded to the nearest dollar and the period tax rounded to the nearest dollar (drop under 50 cents, 50-99 cents up) [US-FED-001]. Social security (OASDI): employee 6.2% and employer 6.2% of wages up to the annual wage base 184,500 USD (effective 2026-01-01 to 2026-12-31); 192,300 USD (effective 2027-01-01 to 2027-12-31) per employee per employer [US-FEDS5-005]. Medicare: employee 1.45% and employer 1.45% of all wages [US-FEDS5-005]. Additional Medicare Tax: 0.9% withheld from the employee on wages above 200,000 USD in the calendar year [US-FEDS5-005]. FUTA (employer only): net rate 0.6% on the first 7,000 USD of wages per employee per year [US-FED-007]. Not configured in this release (open gap WTC-RQ-07): State unemployment insurance and programme definition. | Earning |  | US-TX-01 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes-No | Yes-No | NA |  |  | Yes | Yes |  | US-FED-007, US-FEDP52-005, US-FEDS5-001, US-FEDS5-003, US-FEDS5-005, US-MULTIS5-017 | IRS Publication 15-T (2026) Federal Income Tax Withholding Methods - Worksheet 1A and Annual Percentage Method tables (Automated Payroll) (IRS) [US-FEDS5-001]; IRS Instructions for Form 941 (Rev. Rounding: Optional: wages may be rounded to the nearest dollar and the period tax rounded to the nearest dollar (drop under 50 cents, 50-99 cents up) [US-FED-001]. Social security (OASDI): employee 6.2% and employer 6.2% of wages up to the annual wage base 184,500 USD (effective 2026-01-01 to 2026-12-31); 192,300 USD (effective 2027-01-01 to 2027-12-31) per employee per employer [US-FEDS5-005]. Medicare: employee 1.45% and employer 1.45% of all wages [US-FEDS5-005]. Additional Medicare Tax: 0.9% withheld from the employee on wages above 200,000 USD in the calendar year [US-FEDS5-005]. FUTA (employer only): net rate 0.6% on the first 7,000 USD of wages per employee per year [US-FED-007]. | 62890 | Stock options and grants | Stock options and grants - nonstatutory option spread and restricted stock | Stock options and grants - nonstatutory option spread and restricted stock. Equity compensation (code 62890 nonstatutory option spread and restricted stock; 62990 restricted stock unit; 62991 restricted stock vested; 62993 incentive stock option and employee stock purchase plan income; 62994 section 83(i) inclusion). Resident employee, 2026: Not configured in this release (open gap WTC-RQ-14). Nonresident alien: Nonresident alien employee, 2026: equity income is wages subject to graduated withholding as for residents, with the Pub. 15-T NRA add-on, except that no add-on applies when the payment is withheld at the 22% optional or 37% mandatory flat supplemental rate. Only the U.S.-source part is wages for U.S. withholding: compensation attributable to several years is sourced on a time basis (U.S. workdays / total workdays over the period to which it is attributable, facts and circumstances). FICA/FUTA follow the resident rules (NSO spread and RSU at vesting are FICA wages) unless the employee is exempt as an F-1/J-1/M-1/Q nonimmigrant. Reporting on Form W-2; Form 1042-S only for wages exempt under a treaty claimed on Form 8233. [US-FEDQ1-006]. Restricted stock unit FICA timing: Later of the performance of the services and the date the right is no longer subject to a substantial risk of forfeiture (vesting); FUTA follows the same rule [US-FEDQ1-004]. Incentive stock option and employee stock purchase plan: FICA and FUTA apply: False; FIT withholding required: False [US-FEDQ1-001]. Nonstatutory option income is W-2 box 12 code V [US-FEDQ1-001]. | Earning |  | US-TX-10 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes | Yes-No | Yes-No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes-No | Yes-No | NA |  |  | Yes | Yes | W-2 box 12 code V | US-FEDQ1-001 | IRS Publication 15-B (2026) Employer's Tax Guide to Fringe Benefits - Employee Stock Options; Section 83(i) election (IRS) [US-FEDQ1-001]; Rev. | US-001 | Federal income tax withholding (FIT) - regular wages | Federal income tax withholding (FIT) - regular wages | Federal income tax withheld from the employee on regular wages. Federal income tax (FIT) is withheld by IRS Pub. 15-T (2026) Worksheet 1A with the Annual Percentage Method tables (STANDARD schedule; Step-2-checked schedule when W-4 Step 2 box checked) [US-FEDS5-001]. Rounding: Optional: wages may be rounded to the nearest dollar and the period tax rounded to the nearest dollar (drop under 50 cents, 50-99 cents up) [US-FED-001]. Computation: annualise the period wages with the number of pay periods of the Pay frequency, apply the standard or the Step-2-checked schedule of the percentage method, and divide the annual tax by the same number of periods. Worksheet 1A line 1g, married filing jointly: 12,900 USD [US-FEDS5-001] (effective 2026-01-01 to 2026-12-31); Worksheet 1A line 1g, all other filing statuses: 8,600 USD [US-FEDS5-001] (effective 2026-01-01 to 2026-12-31). Standard schedule bracket thresholds, single: 0/7500/19900/57900/113200/209275/263725/648100 [US-FEDS5-001] (effective from 2026-01-01); bracket rates (percent): 0/10/12/22/24/32/35/37 [US-FEDS5-001] (effective from 2026-01-01). Pre-2020 Form W-4 allowance value: 4,300 USD [US-FEDS5-001]. No usable certificate: Single or Married filing separately in Step 1(c), no entries in Step 2, Step 3 or Step 4 of the 2026 Form W-4 [US-FEDQ3-004]. Extra payday: Pub. 15-T 2026 Worksheet 1A annualises the payroll-period wages by the number of pay periods in Table 3 (weekly 52, biweekly 26); Pub. 15 (2026) section 8: "When you have a regular payroll period, withhold income tax for that time period even if your employee doesn't work the full period"; neither copy contains a provision for a 27th biweekly or 53rd weekly payday (text search of both copies, 2026-11-03). Our reading: the extra payday is withheld as one more ordinary period. FICA wages are taken into account when actually or constructively paid (26 CFR 31.3121(v)(2)-1(a)(1)); FUTA counts remuneration paid in the calendar year up to the $7,000 base (IRC 3306(b)(1)) (effective 2026-01-01 to 2026-12-31); No IRS rule adjusts withholding for a 27th biweekly or 53rd weekly payday: Pub. 15-T Worksheet 1A annualises each payment with the Table 3 count for the payroll period (weekly 52, biweekly 26), and Pub. 15 sec. 8 requires withholding for the regular payroll period, so the extra payday is withheld as one more ordinary period. FICA and FUTA: wages are counted when paid, against the 2026 annual bases for wages paid in 2026. (effective 2026-01-01 to 2026-12-31) [US-FEDQ1-007]. Oracle (Pub. 15-T 2026 worked examples): nonresident alien example result: 31 USD [US-FED-027] (effective from 2026-01-01); tribal example result: 584.09 USD [US-FED-027] (effective from 2026-01-01). Inputs: Form W-4 filing status (Step 1(c)), Form W-4 multiple jobs or spouse works (Step 2), Form W-4 dependents and other credits (Step 3), Form W-4 other income (Step 4(a)), Form W-4 deductions (Step 4(b)), Form W-4 extra withholding (Step 4(c)), Form W-4 exempt from withholding, Pay frequency. Not configured in this release (open gap WTC-RQ-20): the 2027 withholding tables are not published, so only the 2026 tables are configured. Declared: W-2 box 2 and Form 941 line 3 (Report Specifications US-SSA-001 and US-IRS-001). Two-level rule: the federal deduction for qualified tips and overtime operates at assessment and does not change this withholding [US-FEDS5-030]. | Tax |  | US-TX-04 |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  | NA | NA | W-2 box 2; Form 941 line 3; Form 944 line 2 | US-FED-001, US-FED-027, US-FEDQ1-007, US-FEDQ3-004, US-FEDS5-001 | IRS Publication 15-T (2026) Federal Income Tax Withholding Methods - Worksheet 1A and Annual Percentage Method tables (Automated Payroll) (IRS) [US-FEDS5-001]; IRS Instructions for Form 941 (Rev. March 2026) - FICA rates and wage base, Additional Medicare Tax (IRS) [US-FEDS5-005] |
| US-002 | FIT on supplemental wages - optional flat rate | FIT on supplemental wages - optional flat rate | Optional flat-rate withholding on separately identified supplemental wages (bonus, commission, and other payments listed on the supplemental wage rows): 22% [US-FEDS5-003]. Nonresident alien add-on applies to flat-rate supplemental wages: False [US-FEDS5-002]. Table 2 (employee who has submitted Form W-4): weekly 309.6 USD, biweekly 619.2 USD, semimonthly 670.8 USD, monthly 1341.7 USD, annual 16,100 USD [US-FEDS5-002]. Table 1 (no Form W-4): weekly 226.9 USD, annual 11,800 USD [US-FEDS5-002]. Form W-4 rule: Single/MFS, write NRA below 4(c), cannot claim exempt [US-FEDS5-015]. The add-on is used for the computation only; it is not wages and it is not applied to flat-rate supplemental wages. Oracle: the Pub. 15-T 2026 nonresident alien example 31 USD [US-FED-027]. | US-005 | Social security tax (OASDI) - employer | Social security tax (OASDI) - employer | Employer share of the social security tax: 6.2% of social security wages up to the annual wage base 184,500 USD (effective 2026-01-01 to 2026-12-31); 192,300 USD (effective 2027-01-01 to 2027-12-31) per employee per employer [US-FEDS5-005]. Accumulator: year-to-date social security wages of the employee with this employer (Subject to Social Insurance Q1 plus the year-to-date mechanism). Computation: the lesser of (remaining annual maximum) and (rate x period wages), never an annualise-then-divide shortcut. Exempt: Exempt: pay of a nonresident alien in F-1, J-1, M-1 or Q status for services that carry out the purpose of admission, on or off campus, including authorized Curricular and Optional Practical Training. Not exempt: other employment of the same person (e.g. an unrelated part-time job), and any pay once the person is a resident alien. [US-FEDS5-016]; Wages are exempt from employee FICA (IRC 3101(c)) and employer FICA (IRC 3111(c)) while a section 233 Social Security Act agreement is in effect, to the extent the agreement makes them subject exclusively to the foreign social security system. [US-FEDR2-003, US-FEDR2-004]. | US-040 | State of income tax withholding (sourcing flag) | State of income tax withholding (sourcing flag) | Flag that selects the State layer for the employee: the State of work unless reciprocity, the nonresident threshold, the convenience rule, the transport rule or the military spouse rule changes it. Resident credit: credit against tax imposed under the Arizona income tax title for net income taxes paid to the state of residence (A.R.S. 43-1096), applied against Arizona income tax; an assessment-level credit, not a payroll deduction [US-AZWH-003]. Transport: motor carrier residence state only (49 USC 14503); rail residence state only (49 USC 11502); air residence state plus state with >50% of pay (49 USC 40116(f)). Convenience rule: NY (20 NYCRR 132.18), PA (61 Pa. Code 109.8), NE (from 2025) [US-MULTIS5-013]. Mobile workforce bill: not enacted (S.1443, H.R.10271) - do not model. Military spouse: A military spouse present in a State solely to be with the servicemember under military orders neither loses nor acquires residence or domicile; the spouse's income for services performed in that State is not income for services performed or from sources within that State if the spouse is not a resident of it; for any taxable year the spouse may elect the residence of the servicemember, the spouse's own residence or the permanent duty station (50 U.S.C. 4001(a)(2), (c)). Payroll effect: on the employee's State certificate of nonresidence, stop work-State withholding. [US-MULTIS5-005]. Inputs: Work State, Residence State, Interstate transport worker type, Military spouse residence election, State withholding certificate on file. | Flag |  | US-NA |  |  | Recurring |  |  |  | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA | NA |  |  | NA | NA |  | US-AZWH-003, US-FEDP52-005, US-MULTIS5-017 | Not a tax code: no official source applies. |

| US-043 | Cash tips reported by the employee | Cash tips reported by the employee | Cash tips received by the employee and reported to the employer. FIT: withhold on cash tips of 20 or more in a month (tips under 20 a month and non-cash tips: exempt) [US-FEDS5-029]. Social security and Medicare: included for cash tips of 20 or more in a month; excluded under 20 a month [US-FEDS5-029]. FUTA: included for all tips of 20 or more in a month reported in writing to the employer; excluded under 20 a month [US-FEDS5-029]. Reporting: W-2 box 12 code Total amount of cash tips reported to the employer [US-FEDS5-007] and box 14b Treasury Tipped Occupation Code (Primary Treasury Tipped Occupation Code, Secondary Treasury Tipped Occupation Code); assessment (withholding only via W-4 Step 4(b); employer reports W-2 box 12 TP + 14b) [US-FEDS5-030]. Qualified tips deduction cap 25,000 USD at assessment only [US-FEDS5-030]. Washington paid family and medical leave: Tips are excluded from PFML remuneration (RCW 50A.05.010(22)(d)(i)); for UI, wages include tips received while performing services that are employment and reported to the employer for federal income tax purposes (RCW 50.04.320(3)) [US-WAS5-023]. Accumulator: year-to-date tips for the social security wage base (box 7 US-013). Not configured in this release (open gap WTC-RQ-07): State unemployment insurance and programme definition of tips. | Earning |  | US-TX-03 |  |  | Additional |  |  |  | Yes-No | Yes | No | Yes-No | Yes-No | Yes-No | Yes-No | Yes-No | NA |  |  | Yes | Yes | W-2 box 7; box 12 code TP; box 14b | US-FEDS5-007, US-FEDS5-029, US-FEDS5-030 | IRS Publication 15 (2026) sec. 15 Special Rules for Various Types of Services and Payments (table) + sec. 3 family employees + state/local government employers (IRS) [US-FEDS5-029] |

## Sick Leave Paid By Employer

| Employer-paid sick leave - United States |
|---|
| No federal law requires private employers to pay for leave: False [US-FEDS5-026]. Unpaid job-protected family and medical leave under the federal family leave statute lasts up to 12 weeks weeks [US-FEDS5-026] (26 weeks weeks for military caregiver leave); it creates no wage type. |
| Paid sick leave required by a State or city law is paid through the wage rows: the hours taken are paid on the basic salary row 62100 (or the hourly equivalent) and are wages for every tax with the flags of that row. The accrual rates, caps, carry-over and payout rules of each State are in US-CCG-001 v1.0 Module 8 and the State annexes; the catalogue holds no separate statutory sick pay calculation, because there is no employer-paid sickness benefit that follows a reduced assessment base as in the template of another country. |
| Third-party sick pay (an insurer pays) is a flag, not a wage type: see the third-party sick pay flag row of this catalogue. |

## Tax Credit

| Tax credits at payroll - United States |
|---|
| Federal level: the credits of the employee (for example dependents) reach withholding only through Step 3 of the withholding certificate (Form W-4 dependents and other credits (Step 3)); the catalogue holds no credit wage type. The certificate fields are inputs of the federal income tax withholding row. |
| State credits applied inside the State withholding computation (no separate wage type): |
| California withholding allowance credit: 168.3 USD [US-CAS5-001] (effective 2026-01-01 to 2026-12-31) |
| Arkansas personal credit: 29 USD [US-ARS5-001] (effective from 2026-01-01) |
| Delaware personal credit per exemption: 110 USD [US-DES5-006] (effective from 2025-01-01) |
| Oregon allowance credit: 263 USD [US-ORS5-009] (effective 2026-01-01 to 2026-12-31) |

## Global WTC Coverage

| Global WTC coverage - United States |  |  |  |  |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| All 300 pay element codes of the global baseline are present with their codes preserved; country research decides the treatment; 233 country-specific wage types are added (codes US-nnn). Total rows of the catalogue: 533. |  |  |  |  |
| Flag meaning. For an earning row Yes = the amount is wages for that tax (enters its base), No = excluded from the base, Yes-No = conditional (column D states on what), NA = not applicable. Deduction rows follow one of two classes, stated in column D of the row. Class 1, a reversal deduction (the Deduction rows that reverse an earning of the salary, overtime, salary addition, premium, housing and transport families): the row carries the flags of the reversed earning and Yes = the signed amount reduces that base. Class 2, a salary-reduction or pre-tax deduction (retirement plan deferral, health plan contribution): No = removed from the base before the tax. Tax, contribution, flag, reporting and accumulator rows carry NA unless stated. Columns Q1 to Q5 hold one fund each: social security (OASDI), Medicare, FUTA, State unemployment insurance, State disability and paid leave programmes. Column R is NA on every row: the United States has no statutory pension contribution at payroll other than OASDI (Q1). Orange dev-team columns (formula, calculation type, prorating, ignore if 0, enforce, in period reconciliation, year-to-date) are left empty. |  |  |  |  |
| Out of scope: non-employee compensation and backup withholding on it (Form 1099-NEC) [US-OWN-008]; no wage type is built for it. |  |  |  |  |

| Architecture note (sub-national layers). Rows coded US-TX-06, US-TX-07 and US-TX-11 are jurisdiction-specific and mutually exclusive per employee and period: the State income tax row applies for the State chosen by the sourcing flag, reciprocity and the nonresident thresholds replace it, and the resident credit operates at assessment, not as a payroll deduction. The State of unemployment insurance coverage is chosen once per employee by the localization order and the State programmes follow it. Local rows add to, never replace, the State row. |  |  |  |  |

| `fed.equity.withholding_treatment.nonresident` | Nonresident alien employee, 2026: equity income is wages subject to graduated withholding as for residents, with the Pub. 15-T NRA add-on, except that no add-on applies when the payment is withheld at the 22% optional or 37% mandatory flat supplemental rate. | `fed.fit.extra_pay_period.rule` | No IRS rule adjusts withholding for a 27th biweekly or 53rd weekly payday: Pub. 15-T Worksheet 1A annualises each payment with the Table 3 count for the payroll period (weekly 52, biweekly 26), and Pub. 15 sec. 8 requires withholding for the regular payroll period, so the extra payday is withheld as one more ordinary period. FICA and FUTA: wages are counted when paid, against the 2026 annual bases for wages paid in 2026. | from 2026-01-01 to 2026-12-31 | US-FEDQ1-007 |
| `fed.fit.extra_pay_period.rule` | Pub. 15-T 2026 Worksheet 1A annualises the payroll-period wages by the number of pay periods in Table 3 (weekly 52, biweekly 26); Pub. 15 (2026) section 8: "When you have a regular payroll period, withhold income tax for that time period even if your employee doesn't work the full period"; neither copy contains a provision for a 27th biweekly or 53rd weekly payday (text search of both copies, 2026-11-03). Our reading: the extra payday is withheld as one more ordinary period. FICA wages are taken into account when actually or constructively paid (26 CFR 31.3121(v)(2)-1(a)(1)); FUTA counts remuneration paid in the calendar year up to the $7,000 base (IRC 3306(b)(1)) | from 2026-01-01 to 2026-12-31 | US-FEDQ1-007 |
